## Document 335: Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

October 2026
First Home
Buyer Report

### Page 2

2 © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Cotality’s
First Home Buyer
(FHB) Report has tracked
purchasing activity for
several years,
analysing
the
types of properties bought,
the prices paid, and broader
shifts in entry
-
level demand.
The latest edition updates
these measures and extends
the analysis to examine FHB
activity across different
price brackets.
Now in its fourth edition in
partnership with Westpac,
the report also draws on the

bank’s mortgage lending
data to provide additional
insight into borrower
characteristics. That
includes the average age of
FHB borrowers, a measure
not widely available
elsewhere, as well as loan
-
to
-
value ratios across new
mortgages.
For clarity, we define a first home
buyer purchase to be where no party
involved has owned property in New
Zealand before and are using a
mortgage to make the deal.
Note:
The Westpac FHB age and LVR data
are sourced from their lending records

and is not matched to the Cotality
database for this report.
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 3

3
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
FHBs’ market share remains
at record high levels.
In Q3
2026, they accounted for
29.5% of property purchases
across NZ, surpassing the
previous mark of 28.3% only
set three months earlier. The
number of deals remains at
historically high levels too,
bucking the wider trend of
slowing sales volumes for
groups such as investors and
relocating owner
-
occupiers.
This strength for FHBs is
being seen right across the

country,
with market shares
generally running above their
own long
-
term averages
(since 2005). The wider
Wellington area remains a
hotspot for FHBs, accounting
for 37% of activity so far in
2026. But FHBs have also
been at least 30% of the
market in Hamilton and
Auckland, as well as Gisborne,
Rotorua, Hastings,
Palmerston North, and
Invercargill
–
South Waikato
and Waikato District too.
This high level of activity
hasn’t meant too many
compromises either;

standalone houses have
comprised 76% of all FHB
purchases so far in 2026, up
from 75% last year, and the
highest figure since 2020
(78%). To be fair, FHBs have
had to pay a little more, with a
median price so far this year
of $715,000, but still less than
nearly $719,000 in 2022.
FHBs are more active across
the value spectrum too.
For
example, in the middle 40% of
the market by value, their
share rose from 21% in 2015 to
28% in 2025, and now about

30% so far this year.
Access to low deposit
finance under the LVR rules
remains a key support for
first home buyers.
Recent
Reserve Bank data shows that
more than half of FHB loans
are done at less than 20%
deposit (or above 80% LVR),
and Westpac’s own lending
records show a recent
average LVR for FHBs of a
touch more than 80%.
The average age for FHBs on
Westpac’s books has ticked
higher lately, from around 35
late last year to about 35.5
now.
But rather than

necessity due to affordability
pressures, this is more likely
to reflect a choice from many
buyers to just wait for the
right property without fear the
market will run away again in
the meantime.
Looking ahead, although it’s
never easy to get that first
home, conditions should
remain
favourable
for FHBs.
Mortgage rates remain lower
than at many points in the
past, KiwiSaver access for at
least part of the deposit
remains a strong support, and
the banks remain willing and

able to lend at low
deposit/high LVR too.
This is clearly a ‘good
new
s
story’ which we should never
get blasé or complacent
about,
after so many previous
years (even decades) of
intense housing affordability
pressures and a declining
home ownership rate.
As we head into November’s
election, the bottom line is
that first home buyers are
starting from a strong base.
Key findings
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 4

4
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
It’s been another solid few months
for first home buyers with their share
of house sales remaining around
record highs across the country.
That’s a stark contrast to the
softness in the broader housing
market over the past year.
Helping to support those high levels
of first home buyers have been a
couple of big trends. The first is the
large number of properties available
for sale. That’s meant those looking

to get their foot on the property
ladder have had plenty of options. As
a result, first home buyers have often
been able to get into newer and
larger standalone homes without
having to pay significantly more.
The other factor that has boosted
first home buyer numbers has been
the relatively low level of mortgage
rates in recent years. Combined with
broadly flat house prices, that’s
meant servicing a mortgage has
been much more affordable, with the

share of disposable income being
spen
t
on debt
-
servicing costs back
around pre
-
pandemic averages. For
the average first home buyer, lower
mortgage interest rates in recent
years shaved around $560 off their
monthly mortgage payments
compared to 2023 when interest
rates were at their peak. And in
Auckland, where first homes tend to
be more expensive, the saving is
around $685 a month.
While those are encouraging trends
for first home buyers, there are some

big changes in economic conditions
occurring which are likely to have
important implications for the
housing market over the year ahead.
Most notable has been the sharp rise
in inflation in the wake of the Middle
East war, with oil prices rocketing
higher since the start of this year.
That contributed to consumer prices
rising by 3.7% in the year to
September. Relatively high core
inflation levels mean we expect
inflation will push above 4% before

the end of this year and remain high
for much of 2027.
In response to those mounting
inflation pressures, and concerns
that they will persist for some time,
the RBNZ began lifting the Official
Cash Rate (OCR) back in July. We
expect further hikes in coming
months. The resulting pressure on
borrowing costs has seen mortgage
rates push higher in recent months,
with one
-
and two
-
year fixed rates
up around 0.75 percentage points
over the past year.

Even with the recent rise in
mortgage rates, the housing market
is still a lot more accessible for first
home buyers than a few years ago.
But with growing upward pressure
on interest rates, it’s a good time for
prospective first home buyers (or
anyone with a re
-
fix coming up) to
think about the best way to structure
their borrowing. That could help
insulate them from the likely trend
higher in the OCR over the next
couple of years and could save them

a lot of money over the life of their
mortgage. There’s a relatively
modest difference between short
-
and longer
-
term fixed mortgage
rates right now, which means the
cost of taking this ‘insurance’ is not
prohibitive.
Commentary from Satish Ranchhod, Westpac NZ Senior Economist

© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Average age of first home buyers from Westpac loans (2018 to 2026)
Providing some offset to the lift in
borrowing costs, house price
inflation is low, with effectively no
growth in average house prices for
three years now. And with mortgage
rates pushing higher, prices are
expected to remain soft for some
time. We expect that average house
prices will fall by 1.4% over 2026,
with a rise of just 1.8% in 2027.

At the same time, the supply of
housing is continuing to lift, with
more than 40,000 new dwellings
consented over the past year.  We
expect population growth to
increase, but from low levels, which
means the demand for housing might
not be shifting sharply higher any
time soon.
That combination of conditions
means that there is still likely to be a
lot of options
f
or
those looking to get
into their first home over the coming
year.
Cotality – Westpac

First Home Buyer Report
Source: Cotality, Westpac

### Page 6

6
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The national picture
Since our previous FHB Report
back in May 2026, the wider
housing market has remained
subdued and patchy
–
generally
sales volumes have been flat to
falling, while property values have
been showing a similarly soft
picture, albeit not everywhere.
Amongst the main
centres
,
Auckland and Wellington, as
examples, have been more sluggish
than say Christchurch or Dunedin.
It’s also noteworthy that the stock

of listings available for sale remains
elevated across the country.
It’s not difficult to find the reasons
why the market remains in the
doldrums.
The economic backdrop
is still uncertain, the unemployment
rate is above average, and
mortgage rates have been drifting
higher in recent months as inflation
fears remain strong. A better
balance of physical housing supply
versus our population is also
dampening prices, while the
election has been a recent reason

for caution too. It’s harder to
measure, but ‘animal spirits’ aren’t a
key factor
at the moment
either.
FHBs have gone from strength to
strength
In this purchaser
-
friendly
environment, first home buyers
have continued to take full
advantage.
The Cotality Buyer
Classification data shows that their
market share hit a new quarterly
record high of 28.3% in Q2 2026,
only to push higher still in Q3
–
reaching 29.5%. They’re also faring
well in terms of the number of deals

too, recently hovering at around
25,000 on a 12
-
month rolling basis,
back to levels last seen (for a brief
period) at the height of the post
-
COVID boom.
Clearly, lower property values
–
still down by more than 18%
nationally from the peak
–
and
improved housing affordability
(even despite some recent rises in
mortgage rates) are helping FHBs.
But those aren’t the only factors;
access to (ever
-
growing) KiwiSaver
pots for at least part of the deposit

is also a factor, alongside decent
availability of high LVR finance at
the banks.
Indeed, the 25% allowance/speed
limit to lend to owner
-
occupiers at
less than 20% equity (or >80% LVR)
is not being tested
at the moment
and recently more than half of FHB
loans have been advanced at less
than 20% deposit.
Westpac’s own
mortgage lending records show
this in a different way
–
with the
average LVR for their loans to
FHBs in recent months still
running a touch above 80%.

LVRs
tend to be higher in Auckland, but a
bit lower in Canterbury and
Wellington.
They’re also getting more for their
money
FHBs have always tended to be
willing to compromise on location
or property type to make the shift
from renting into ownership, but
lately there hasn’t been much need
to take such an approach.
Over
2026 to date (Q3), standalone
houses have comprised 76% of all
FHB purchases, up from 75% last
year, and well above 2023’s
cyclical trough of less than 70%.

For comparison, standalone houses
have accounted for around 72% of
deals across all
buyers
year to date.
N
ational
FHB activity

This shows that FHBs are getting 
the most for their money in a 
quieter market. Admittedly, the 
median price they’ve paid so far 
this year has risen to $715,000, up 
from $701,000 in 2025 
 and also  
higher than the 2023 ($695,000) 
and 2024 figures ($692,000). But 
that latest price is still around 
$4,000 lower than in 2022, yet 
FHBs are still getting ‘more house’.
Another important point to make is 
that FHBs don’t always enter at the 
bottom of the housing ladder and 
work their way up 
 – 
many 
 actually 
enter  
well above the lowest tiers of 
the market. Indeed, the FHB 
median price paid of $715,000 this 
year is below the all
 -
 buyer figure of 
$785,000, but well above the all
 -
buyer lower quartile (bottom 25%) 
of $595,000. 
This point can be made another 
way too, with Cotality figures 
showing a rising share of activity 
for FHBs right across the price 
spectrum. Perhaps most notably, 
FHBs have accounted for 30% of 
purchases in the middle 40% price 
bracket of transactions so far this 
year, up from a 28% share in 2025, 
25% in 2020, and only 21% in 2015. 
They’ve even accounted for 15% of 
activity so far this year in the top 
30% of deals split by price.
FHBs’ average age has edged 
higher again, but probably just due 
to a lack of pressure
Meanwhile, at face value, 
Westpac’s data showing that the 
average age of 
 a 
FHB has recently 
edged back up to around 2024 
levels of 35.5 again, after a trough 
of 35 late last year, could seem at 
odds with the other, more 
encouraging trends. But rather than 
being out of necessity (
 e.g.  
due to 
affordability pressures), this could 
reflect the abundance of listings 
and subdued house prices, 
meaning that 
 some/many FHBs 
might just be choosing to wait a 
bit longer to get their ideal 
property, without fear they’ll be 
left behind by a rising market.

A solid outlook
Overall, it’s never easy to buy that 
first home and FHBs certainly 
continue to face challenges. But 
their presence has remained strong 
in recent months and, although 
nothing lasts forever, it’s not hard 
to imagine that FHBs stay a solid 
presence into 2027 as well. This is 
a ‘good 
 news 
 story
 ’ which we 
should never get 
 blasé 
 or 
complacent about, after so many 
previous years (decades) of intense 
housing affordability pressures and 
a declining home ownership rate.

The main centres
Looking at the data across
Auckland, Hamilton, Tauranga,
wider Wellington (i.e. combined
City, Lower Hutt, Upper Hutt,
Porirua), Christchurch, and
Dunedin, the national trends can
also be seen at the more granular
level
–
indeed, all of these areas
have had FHB market shares lately
that are above their own averages.
The wider Wellington area remains
a standout for FHBs, where their

share so far this year has been a
touch above 37%, well
in excess of
their long
-
term average in that
market of 30%. FHBs have also had
a market share in 2026 above 30%
in Hamilton and Auckland. The
figures have been a little lower in
Christchurch and Dunedin (both
around 28%), as well as Tauranga
(circa 25%), but in each of those
markets the figures have still been
4
-
8%
-
points above their own
averages.
Looking at the different property
types, standalone houses still

dominate in most areas, accounting
for 85
-
90% of FHB purchases in
Dunedin, Tauranga, and Hamilton
–
broadly in line with the
long term
norms in those areas. The share for
houses amongst FHB purchases is
always lower in Christchurch,
Wellington, and Auckland, but it’s
perhaps the former that stands out
most at present
–
with only 66% of
FHB purchases so far in 2026 in
Christchurch being standalone
houses, versus the historical
average of 75%. This no doubt

reflects the recent townhouse
construction upswing.
In each main
centre
, the median
FHB price is lower than the figure
for all buyers, but higher than the
lower quartile for all buyers
–
in
other words, FHBs don’t always
enter at the bottom. In terms of the
prices themselves, Auckland is the
main
centre
where FHBs pay the
most, at a median of $880,000 so
far in 2026, which is $110,000 less
than the all
-
buyer median, but
$110,000 more than the all

-
buyer
lower quartile. Median FHB prices
paid are $795,500 in Tauranga so
far in 2026, followed by Wellington
at $736,250, Hamilton $715,000,
and lower still in Christchurch and
Dunedin.

The main urban areas
Digging deeper into the next 12 key
centres
around New Zealand, the
story of relative strength for FHBs’

so far in 2026. Palmerston North
stands out with 33% of
transactions in the year to date
going to FHBs (6%
-
points above its
average), although Rotorua,
Gisborne, Hastings, and Invercargill
have also had FHB shares of 30%
or more this year.  Relative to their

own averages, Napier and Hastings
have been strongest, with figures
9%
-
points above normal.
But even in areas where FHBs have
traditionally found conditions
harder or have simply been less
interested, there’s still been relative
strength this year. For example,
Whangarei’s share going to FHBs
has been 25% in 2026 (versus
average since 2005 of 19%), Kapiti
Coast at 21% versus 17%, and
Queenstown
-
Lakes at 19% versus
15%.
Standalone houses tend to be the

dominant type of property amongst
the existing dwelling stock in many
provincial areas, and therefore also
account for a high share of FHB
purchases. So far this year, houses
have been at least 90% of FHB
purchases in Invercargill,
Palmerston North, Whanganui,
Rotorua, and Gisborne. It’s been
80
-
89% in Nelson, Kapiti Coast,
Hastings, Whangarei, Napier, and
New Plymouth. Queenstown is
lower at just 64%, but that’s likely
to reflect a wider range of property

types amongst the existing stock of
dwellings, as well as affordability
pressures which are probably
pushing FHBs towards lower price
brackets (and hence smaller
dwellings).
Indeed, in terms of median prices
paid by FHBs so far this year, the
highest figure (amongst the main
urban areas) has been in
Queenstown, at $1,275,000. Kapiti
Coast comes in at $700,000, with
Whangarei, Rotorua, Napier,
Hastings, New Plymouth, and
Nelson all at least $600,000. By

contrast, Invercargill ($480,000)
and Whanganui ($460,000) are at
the lower end.

## Document 356: Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports
Europe’s auto deal with China is a good start. But containing China’s export boom
will require a much broader response.
European Commissioner for Trade and Economic Security Maros Sefcovic speaks at a press briefing at the EU Delegation to
China in Beijing, China October 9, 2026. Florence Lo/Reuters
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
November 2, 2026 11:02 a.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The year-over-year increase in Chinese automobile
exports to all markets is currently about 3.5 million
cars.
Over ten years, that is 35 million cars. The global
market excluding China is only around 60 million
cars. Japan raised its auto exports by about 3.5 million
cars in the 1970s—but that was over a decade, not
one year. And right now the U.S. and Indian markets

(around 20 million cars) are effectively closed to
Chinese production. The 2026 jump in exports is an
incredible.
China’s domestic market, though, is down 20 percent
this year—that works out to a decline of about 5
million cars. The Wall Street Journal estimates that
domestic sales will fall to around 19 million cars this
year, less than a third of the production capacity of
China’s auto industry.
So even though China’s exports are increasing at an

unprecedented pace, China’s domestic auto sector
isn’t really booming; production is essentially flat, and
intense domestic competition is destroying the
profitability of firms that don’t export.*

### Page 3

3
Europe’s new auto deal with China should be
understood in this context.
The deal limits China’s exports of hybrids (including
plug-in hybrids) to Europe to around 350,000 cars.
That is above the 300,000 hybrids that China
exported to Europe in 2025, but well below the (gulp)
800,000 annualized pace for hybrids in the last three
months of (European) data. Total imports,
annualized, were running at an incredible 2 million a
year pace thanks to strong battery electric imports in

September.

### Page 4

4
China appears to have concluded that it prefers a
guaranteed market for a limited number of cars (and
more or less guaranteed profits) to the risk of a
prohibitive tariff that would cut off all its exports.
And Europe preferred a negotiated deal to the risk of
retaliation and a slowdown in licensing rare earth and
magnet exports.
The Commission also thinks of this as a bit of a
model—more on that later.
I certainly understand the logic of the deal.

Something needed to be done. China’s export of
hybrids to Europe were up an insane 200 percent
year-over-year in the last 3 months of data, which led
to a 75 percent increase for all cars. One more year of
75 percent growth would bring China’s exports to

### Page 5

5
around 3 million cars; in two years, close to 6 million,
and in three years, 10 million—or most of the market.
But Europe still is a long way from a coherent trade
and industrial policy with respect to autos, let alone a
coherent economic policy towards overall trade with
China.
After this deal, as I understand it:
Hybrid imports are capped at 350,000, down
from a projected 800,000 in 2026—and then
rising to 370,000 and 390,000 in subsequent
years.

Battery electric vehicles face a tariff from 25
percent (Telsa, 10 percent base + 15 percent) to
45 percent (10 percent base plus 35 percent).
BYD’s total tariff is at 27 percent, which isn’t
prohibitive. Even with these tariffs, battery
electric imports will total around 600,000 this
year, up from 500,000 last year.
Diesel and gasoline (ICE) cars face a tariff of
10 percent. Europe is importing around
200,000 of these a year (including a significant

number from Chinese-owned MG).
There are no special tariffs on imported parts or
imported batteries, which matters for the growing
number of Chinese factories in Europe. So long as
tariffs are low and the renminbi is undervalued,
Chinese firms still have an incentive to maximize the

### Page 6

6
Chinese content of their “transplant” factories (ask
Thailand).
There is currently no unified European policy on
inward investment that requires joint ventures or
technology transfer either, only a lot of wishful
thinking.**
Sum it up, and it is a market where Chinese exports
are constrained by a hodge-podge of different trade
defense tools, and there is no standard policy for
ensuring that Chinese assembly operations use
European parts rather than import Chinese kits. The

existing operations, which are just starting to scale up,
don’t draw on a European supply chain. They likely
preserve a few assembly jobs at the expense of the
broader automotive ecosystem.
A coherent European (or national) industrial policy
for the automotive and battery sectors is thus still a
work in progress.
The safeguard on hybrids and the resulting tariff rate
quota only stops the bleeding.
And make no mistake, Europe’s position in the global

automotive sector is slipping. Europe still runs an
overall surplus in auto trade, but that surplus is falling
fast if trade in batteries is added to automotive trade
(there isn’t a split between batteries used for energy

### Page 7

7
storage and batteries used by cars in the EU data
right now, at least not that I have found).
Europe’s broader trade policy to China is similarly a
work in progress.
The surge in imports of hybrid cars was particularly
pronounced, but it is by no means the only such surge.
The balance in industrial machinery has eroded
significantly. Consider the following chart, which
shows imports and exports of machinery, electronics
and vehicles (HS 84, 85, and 87) by weight.

### Page 8

8
The balance in chemicals, measured by weight to
abstract from price changes, shows the same trend.
Europe doesn’t have an across-the-board tool for
applying tariffs against China like the U.S. section
301 statute.

### Page 9

9
But that isn’t necessarily an immediate constraint, as
there are plenty of solid safeguard cases thanks to the
current surge in European imports from China.
But if each safeguard needs to include a negotiated
tariff-rate quota that gives China enough guaranteed
market access (and Chinese firms enough guaranteed
profits) to ensure trade peace, Europe needs to get
moving with negotiation in other sectors.
Chemicals and machinery are more diverse sectors

than cars and thus aren’t as amenable to the same
high-level Š ef č ovi č -Wang negotiations the led to the
most recent deal.
This critique isn’t meant as criticism. Europe needed
to act on cars. Look at a chart of automotive trade in
kilos.

### Page 10

10
It’s just that it also needs to act in other sectors: auto
parts, batteries (though that is complicated),
chemicals, and some categories of industrial
machinery. The scale of China’s export boom and the
fact that in most sectors there are no limits to China’s
ability to scale up is a real problem.***
And Europe cannot wait for China to conclude that
it would rather grow on the basis of internal, rather
than external, demand.
I disagree with the IMF’s former top brass on this. A

China that grows on the back of a 1.5 pp net export
contribution for another couple of years will
deindustrialize much of the world. China’s trading
partners cannot step aside and do nothing until
China figures out how to escape deflation using its
own demand. Xi appears more than happy to
continue with his current policy mix and gamble that

### Page 11

11
the world will continue to absorb China’s ever
growing export surplus.
Pascal Lamy, Nicolas Köhler-Suzuki, and Victor Luiz
do Prado of the Jacques Delors Institute recognize
the need for a more systemic response—as do Jean-
Pierre Landau and Sébastien Jean, who have done an
excellent job explaining how China’s micro- and
macro-industrial policies fit together into a coherent,
self-reinforcing whole.
The proposals in the Lamy et al. paper overlap heavily

with the Commission’s current approach to China.
The authors envision a two-pronged approach: first,
negotiating with China to seek agreement on tariff-
rate quotas that effectively limit the EU’s imports
from China; and second, developing a broad yet
targeted tariff-based instrument to address structural
imbalances should negotiations fail. The latter would
give Europe a credible threat should China refuse to
agree to the proposed safeguards.
My own view: don’t let the perfect be the enemy of

the good.
The EU has shied away from developing its own 301
tool out of concern about preserving a “rules-based
system.” I still think a European 301 would allow a
more fulsome response, but understand that
European isn’t as willing as the United States to use

### Page 12

12
tools that at least appear to be outside those
authorized by the WTO. In reality the EU no longer
really wants to trade with China on the same (MFN)
terms applied to other WTO members. That mirrors
the current view of the United States. But the EU is
willing to go through more procedural hoops to get to
a similar result.****
But I also recognize that, absent policy adjustment in
China—notably a stronger currency that allows for
fair market competition—the scope of safeguards and

managed trade will progressively expand.
* One sign: Stellantis is resuming Jeep production in
China, but primarily for export rather than the
domestic market.
** There is a lot of (in my view) somewhat confused
analysis of Chinese investment that works off the
analogy provided by either foreign investment in
China’s auto sector or Japanese investment in the U.S.
and Europe in the 1980s. These are relevant examples
of course, but asserting that Chinese investment will

result in a similar outcome when the underlying
economics differ is a big logical jump. Japanese firms
had incentives to localize their parts production in the
United States from the then-strong yen as well as
from the risk of protection. Foreign firms operating in
China had to do JVs until recently and had incentives
to localize from China’s significant tariff on auto

### Page 13

13
parts and China’s lower cost structure (in part because
of a weak RMB). Tesla’s Shanghai incentives and tax
deal included strong localization incentives, and there
were additional incentives from Tesla’s desire to
qualify for the lists the governed access to China’s
consumer EV subsidies. That is why something like
90 percent of Tesla parts, including its batteries, are
now made in China. Chinese investors in Europe
have no similar incentives to localize right now, yet

many assume that the results will be the same. That,
to me, is naïve. Since the underlying economics differ
(Europe’s cost basis is much higher than existing
production in China) getting Chinese firms to do
more than final assembly in Europe will take stronger
policy tools than were required to get foreign firms to
localize parts production in China. Europe’s
Industrial Accelerator Act would be a step in the
right direction, but more is likely needed. Thailand

provides a useful cautionary example.
*** China can make over 60 million cars, according to
recent reporting from the Wall Street Journal. The
domestic market this year will be under 20 million
cars (down from something like 24 million cars last
year). Even with exports of 10 million, there is ~30
million cars worth of unused auto production
capacity (roughly double European demand). And
China is still investing at a pace that is consistent
with adding close to 5 million of EV production

capacity a year. Chinese firms, who don’t make any
money on domestic sales, are strongly incentivized to

### Page 14

14
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.
From Follow the Money and Greenberg Center for Geoeconomics
export. The world has never encountered such elastic
supply.
**** The more different the legal basis for the
European and U.S. response—and the more policy
differences (TRQs vs. a flat tariff, for example) the

harder it would be to ever develop a harmonized
policy approach. But with President Trump, a
harmonized policy isn’t even a U.S. goal.

### Page 15

## Document 402: Monthly household spending indicator_ Spending resilient despite headwinds (2026-11-06)

Date: 2026-11-06

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
HOUSEHOLD SPENDING
INDICATOR BULLETIN
Spending resilient despite headwinds
4 November 2026
Key points
• Household spending rose 0.6%mth in
September, returning to growth after
August’s brief decline. Q3 spending increased
2.1%qtr, while volumes rose a more subdued

0.7%qtr, pointing to a sharp acceleration
in price growth through the quarter.
• Services spending rebounded and goods
spending remained supportive. Non-
discretionary spending outperformed
discretionary categories, with higher fuel
spending providing significant support.
• In real terms, spending growth over the
quarter was led by transport but with
most other categories also recording
gains. All states except Tasmania
recorded positive volume growth

in Q3, led by WA and Victoria.
• Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in
line with expectations and consistent with
our consumption outlook ahead of the Q3
National Accounts (due 2 December).
Q3 real household
spending:
0.7%qtr, 3.2%yr

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel  which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.
Spending returns to growth in September

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts 
are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
US-China talks, a possible Trump-Xi-Putin trilateral meeting, and any signs of a China-Japan diplomatic
thaw will be critical watchpoints. The priorities of the summit between representatives of 21 Asia-Pacific
economies are set to include efforts to boost innovation, digital connectivity, supply-chain resilience and

support for an open multilateral trading system. During the summit, presidents Trump and Xi are expected to
meet for the third time this year. This meeting will provide signals on the state of China-US talks, including the
operationalisation of new bilateral trade and investment boards, and on the likelihood of another extension of
the Busan trade truce beyond January 2027. President Trump has also indicated he is open to a trilateral US-
China-Russia meeting during the summit, possibly covering nuclear arms control and the Russia-Ukraine war.

Investors will also closely watch whether Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi and President Xi meet on the
sidelines, following reports that Tokyo is hoping to arrange a meeting. However, the bar for a formal
breakthrough will likely remain high. As we noted in a report earlier this year, a full normalisation of relations
and an easing of Chinese economic restrictions – which include rare-earth export controls – may require a
lengthy period of diplomatic re-engagement and backchannel diplomacy.

A divided government could mean less change at home but more abroad. Third-party polling and betting
markets forecast Democrat control of the House, and an increasing probability of flipping the Senate. 80% of
respondents in our monthly poll now say living costs will influence their vote – the most since April. For
markets, a divided government could mean less domestic legislative change, but not necessarily a more
predictable US foreign policy. Losing Congress could leave theadministration’s geopolitical and geoeconomic

toolkit largely intact.
In our view, weapons stockpiles, inflationary pressures and long-end yields may matter more than Congress
in constraining theadministration’s choices in 2027 and 2028. A Democratic House could dial up oversight of
the administration, launch investigations, force votes on War Powers Resolutions, and use appropriations bills
to press for restrictions on departmental funding. This may generate a steady stream of attention-grabbing

headlines, but whether it decisively affects the event-driven risk that matters for markets (e.g. blocking a tariff
or preventing a limited military strike) remains in doubt. If domestic policymaking becomes more challenging,
foreign policy could become a top priority for the Trump administration.

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
NewZealand’s general election is shaping up as a close and fragmented contest. Both the ruling National
and opposition Labour parties are polling at historically low levels, and smaller parties will likely determine
the next government. In a closely contested vote, investors could see coalition uncertainty and a wider

range of possible outcomes for tax, spending, infrastructure, energy and migration policy.
The National party is campaigning on a platform that would broadly preserve the current emphasis on
fiscal restraint, lower regulation and private-sector investment. A Labour-led government could place
greater weight on public services, housing and climate policy, alongside a proposed capital-gains tax on
investment property. However, because any government will rely on minor parties for survival, the final

policy mix will depend heavily on post-election negotiations.
The meeting will be a test of the EU’s ability to translate the competitiveness agenda into practical
reforms. The focus will be on Europe’s ability to restore competitiveness, mobilize investment and
maintain fiscal discipline. The Eurogroup brings together Euro-area finance ministers and is expected to
consider macroeconomic and fiscal developments, energy market pressures and progress towards the

Savings and Investments Union. ECOFIN brings together all EU finance ministers, and will focus on
financial integration, banking sector competitiveness, and economic coordination. To watch will be
tensions between fiscal consolidation and the need to fund defence, energy security and strategic
investment, alongside progress on integratingEurope’s fragmented capital markets and banking sectors. A
budget-focused Council meeting follows on 10 November.

Implementation of existing commitments, rather than new policy pledges, is expected to be the primary
focus of the next climate summit in Antalya. Building on the shift towards implementation at COP30,
COP31 will focus on translating existing climate commitments into practical action, including through
investment, financing and partnerships. For investors, the key watchpoint will be whether the summit can
deliver tangible commitments that translate existing plans into binding funding pledges for critical

infrastructure resilience, scaled-up renewable energy output, and climate finance.

### Page 5

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
Melaka’s state election will offer a further gauge of Malaysia’s evolving electoral landscape after
strong recent state-level performances by Barisan Nasional (BN). The contest will also be watched for
its read-through to Prime Minister Anwar Ibrahim’s Pakatan Harapan (PH), following setbacks in other

state elections this year.
BN enters the election from a position of relative strength, while its electoral understanding with PAS
and its allies could provide an early indication of how political alignments are evolving ahead of the next
general election. However, Melaka’s local dynamics mean the result should not be treated as a direct
national forecast.
The anticipated signing of a landmark digital economy agreement is set to be one of the most

significant summit deliverables in years. The 49th ASEAN Summit in Manila will focus on economic
integration, energy security, supply-chain resilience, maritime security, the crisis in Myanmar, and
relations with external partners.
For markets, the formal signing of the ASEAN Digital Economy Framework Agreement (DEFA) is the key
event to watch. Designed as a legally binding treaty, DEFA aims to harmonize regional digital trade rules
and cross-border data flows. Successful implementation is expected to double the projected size of the

region’s digital economy by 2030, boosting it from a projected baseline of US$1 trillion to US$2 trillion
(up from approximately US$300 billion today).
The second EU-UK Summit will be an important test of whether the post-Brexit relationship can move
towards tangible outcomes. Prime Minister Andy Burnham will seek to finalize agreements on food and
agricultural standards, youth mobility, and linking emissions trading systems. Burnham has framed
relations as “not good enough.” To watch will be whether we see concrete deals versus declarations, and

whether leaders are prepared to make the concessions necessary to deepen ties.

### Page 6

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
The upcoming local elections could serve as a political barometer ahead of the 2028 general election–
but only up to a point. Local elections in Taiwan are primarily driven by municipal ‘bread and butter’
economic issues; geopolitics and cross-Strait relations have historically not been at the forefront of

voters’ concerns. While this divergence limits theelections’ predictive value for the January 2028 general
vote, the results can provide a sense of the domestic balance of power.
Elections in Taiwan’s six special municipalities – Taipei, New Taipei, Taichung, Tainan, Taoyuan, and
Kaohsiung – will serve as particularly important watchpoints. Some recent polling suggests a potentially
close contest in Taipei between incumbent mayor Chiang Wan-an (Kuomintang) and legislator Shen Po-

yang (Democratic Progressive Party).
Spain’s snap general election could mark a major turning point for Spanish politics and the EU,
potentially ending eight years of Socialist-led government under Pedro Sánchez and bringing the
conservative People’s Party (PP) to power with support from the far-right Vox. Polling suggests PP is
ahead and a PP-Vox alignment is the only one projected to reach a majority. A rightward shift in one of
the EU’s largest economies could occur just as Europe enters a wider electoral cycle, potentially

complicating consensus on migration, climate policy, Ukraine and European integration.
The Forum comes at a pivotal moment in EU-Latin American relations. It is the EU’s first big commercial
push into Argentina since the EU-Mercosur trade deal began to be provisionally applied on 1 May. The
agreement opens new opportunities for trade, investment and market access. The EU is Argentina’s
largest source of foreign direct investment. Around 800 government, business and financial leaders will

focus on energy, critical minerals, agriculture and infrastructure. The EU’s Global Gateway investment
agenda provides a framework for mobilizing public and private capital. While a key outcome will be the
launch of a structured EU-Argentina investment dialogue, to watch will be the ability of Argentine
officials to allay concerns around policy stability and regulatory predictability.

### Page 7

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
A potential Trump-Kim meeting in November could restart official bilateral dialogue, though North
Korea remains highly unlikely to abandon its nuclear program. South Korea’s National Intelligence
Service said in late September that it saw a roughly 50% chance of US-North Korea talks around the
APEC Summit in Shenzhen, although more recent reporting suggests the prospects may have

diminished. If talks do resume, they would only mark the first step in a long and highly uncertain
diplomatic process.
As we noted earlier this year, North Korea is highly unlikely to agree to denuclearization. Unless renewed
negotiations sidestep denuclearization in favor of a risk-reduction or coexistence framework, dialogue is
likely to stall, not least given Pyongyang’s constitutional pledge to continue expanding its nuclear
arsenal. This would make even a negotiated freeze on further expansion difficult to achieve.

電子製造業 ESG 評等分配圖

瑞昱 2379 TT  ( 電子上游–IC 設計 ) 
網通規格逐步推進，待評價面更具吸引力 
投資與評價 
1. PC 表現雖較為疲弱，但受惠基建升級與高頻寬解決方案的挹注下，網通
產品需求優於預期，預估 26 年營收年增 21.8% 
2. 規格升級仍將為主要驅動力，AI 產品亦添柴火，但推估傳統應用在高基
期下成長性有限，預估 27 年營收年增 9.7%。 
3. 預估瑞昱 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 31.91/38.15 元，考量成長動能及金融
環境不確定性，以 27 年獲利預估乘上本益比區間上緣 20 倍為評價基
礎，目標價 760 元(20X FY27E EPS)，股價尚未具顯著吸引力，維持中
立投資評等。 
重點更新 
網通產品維持韌性，表現略優於預期，預估 2026 營收年增 21.8% 
網通需求受惠基礎建設持續升級與高頻寬解決方案的挹注，其中乙太網路隨
路由器、中小企業網路及伺服器採用擴大而成長，網管型交換器在企業、中
小企業及伺服器控制層應用持續提升市占 ， 與非網管型產品的差距逐漸縮小 。
Wi-Fi 方面，Wi-Fi 7 在 PC 與路由器的滲透率，預估今年約達 30%，在車用
乙太網路採用持續穩定擴大，今年營收亦呈穩定成長態勢。但在消費性電子
方面，因 DRAM 與 CPU 等零組件持續漲價推升零售價格，削弱消費性與入
門級購買力，提前拉貨效應亦趨緩，使得PC 表現研究部預估年減約一成，另
外電視出貨在年中世足賽備貨後放緩，零組件成本壓力也持續存在 。綜合考
量下，整體預估瑞昱 2026 營收年增 21.8%達 1494.6 億元。 
2027 年規格升級及 AI 為主要驅動力，但評價仍不具吸引力 
Wi-Fi 方面，Wi-Fi 7 的滲透率，預估 2027 年將達 40%，新一代 Wi-Fi 8 開
發進度順利，預計 2H27 量產，乙太網路與 10G PON 採用擴大，帶動產品
組合改善，高階網管型交換器預估也將取得更多 design win。目前伺服器產
品涵蓋管理連接與周邊功能，包括乙太網路與交換器、 SSD 控制器、 I/O 
bridge 及 USB hub 控制器，並以此為基礎進入更高速的伺服器互連，推估
2027 年營收佔比將達一成水準 。100G 光通訊解決方案正進行認證，預期
2027 年放量更明顯，400G 與 800G 解決方案開發則穩定進行，預估 2H27
推出，PC 則預估呈現持平表現。整體而言，預估 27 年營收年增 9.7%，達
1639.1 億元，以評價面來考量，目前股價仍尚未具顯著吸引力。

資料來源：台灣指數公司，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 2

訪談內容 
網通產品規格升級+AI 將為長期成長動能 
2026 年 Wi-Fi 7 在 PC/NB 市場滲透率達 30%，約為 2025 年的兩倍，高階商用機型導入最積
極，Wi-Fi 聯盟目標 2027 年 6 月前完成認證測試計畫，並於 2027 年 9 月正式啟動 Wi-Fi 8 認
證。正積極與客戶進行Wi-Fi 8 design-in，預期產品 2H27 開始放量，客戶產品將在正式認證後
於 2028 年更廣泛上市。Wi-Fi 8 在 Wi-Fi 7 的基礎上著重實際使用環境中的極高可靠度，以及更
低、更穩定的延遲，預估將成為廣泛採用且生命周期長的平台。

車用乙太網路在新車生產的滲透率已超過 50%，全球汽車出貨持平，成長並非依賴車輛出貨增
加，而是來自單車乙太網路埠數結構性增加、持續打入新車廠客戶，以及穩定的 design win 動
能。隨車廠電子電機架構演進，需求自然轉向更高密度的安全關鍵解決方案，產品路線圖與此趨
勢一致，正擴充更高整合度、更多埠數的車用交換器，並開發先進閘道控制器，以因應未來軟體
定義汽車的需求。

另外 AI ASIC 機會目前處於與客戶接觸的初期階段 ， 基礎為高速連接與介面IP 的優勢 。 邊緣AI(含
地端伺服器與邊緣伺服器)是推動規格升級、提升單機價值的結構性機會，而非僅依賴PC 需求復
甦的成長動能。邊緣 AI 對更高頻寬、更低延遲、更佳功耗效率與更豐富多模態互動的需求，可
為產品組合創造更高價值的解決方案。

供應端部分預期 2027 年改善將較明顯，工程端也導入 design for availability 原則，在可行範
圍內降低對受限物料的依賴。成本漲幅已超過可合理吸收的程度，與客戶逐案討論價格調整。

表一、瑞昱 2027 修正預估與前次預估比較

### Page 3

瑞昱 ESG 政策 
瑞昱主要從事 IC 設計，產業類別歸類在半導體行業中，依據專門為投資人設計的永續揭露框架 SASB
準則，半導體行業關注重點為下列幾項：溫室氣體排放、能源管理、水和廢水管理、廢棄物和危險材料
管理、員工健康與安全、員工多元化和包容性、產品設計與生命週期管理、材料採購與效率。由於瑞昱
為 IC 設計公司，研究部將篇幅著重在產品設計面，並因員工對於IC 設計公司而言是相當重要的資產，
故員工相關議題也會著重於下描述，其餘溫室氣體排放及產品設計與生命週期管理等則於後簡述。

1. 員工健康與安全： 
瑞昱重視及關心同仁的身體健康與生活狀態，以優於法令之規定，每年定期實施免費健檢服務。對於健
康檢查結果為重大 ( 含輕度 ) 異常同仁，依異常項目、病情及個人健康情形，於複檢前、後分別安排
廠醫諮詢及衛教說明，持續追蹤健檢結果。與新竹臺大分院新竹醫院合作之健康諮詢服務，每月安排 9 
場次駐廠醫師健康諮詢服務，每場次由專業醫師提供  3 小時諮詢服務，2022 年度共計服務 384 人
次。

圖 1 歷年健檢人數統計

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 員工多元化和包容性： 
2022 年瑞昱全集團全球員工人數為 6,828 人，海外員工註人數佔總 員工人數約 36.9%，其中 72% 
以上同仁擁有碩士、博士學歷。瑞昱半導體以臺灣為主要營運據點， 臺灣地區員工人數為 4,943 人，
其中正式員工人數 4,826 人，約聘員工人數 117 人，男性員工所佔 比例約 80.46%、女性員工所佔
比例約 19.54%，其中 77% 以上同仁擁有碩士、博士學歷。在 2022 年所聘僱的人力中，無論在正
式員工、男性員工、女性員工等類別指標，皆有明顯成長。 
圖 2 近三年員工人數

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

3. 溫室氣體排放： 
瑞昱在 2022 年溫室氣體排放共計 26,462.8729 tCO2e ，其中直接排放(範疇 1)為 808.4856 tCO2e，
分別為固定排放源(緊急發電機之柴油)、移動排放源(公務車之汽油)及逸散排放源(冷媒、化糞池、滅火
器)，瑞昱因無生產製程，故無製程排放源；間接排放(範疇 2)為 25654.3873 tCO2e，主要來源為外購
電力，占整體排放量 96.9%。

圖 3 溫室氣體排放量、強度

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水和廢水管理： 
瑞昱於 2022 年持續成功開發出多項新一代產品，在各種應用功耗 （瓦數） 表現上比前一代產品功耗減
少、面積也縮小，在長時間及大量傳輸時更具顯著降低耗能之效果，充份體現節能與對環境之友善，並
帶給終端用戶更佳的使用體驗。

圖 4 產品設計成效

資料來源：瑞昱半導體、國泰證期研究部整理

## Document 520: 【科技產業攻略】科技財報仍以ＡＩ展望最樂觀

1
科技財報仍以AI展望最樂觀
分析師／張乘維、謝孟璇、張偉漢
研究範疇／科技
科技產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/30
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI晶片與相關硬體獲利持續創新高》
根據機構LSEG統計，受到AI晶片、記憶體、硬碟等
科技公司獲利優於預期，機構上調科技產業4Q26、
1Q27獲利年增率到56.2%、54.6%，本行樂觀看待
科技產業發展後勢 。10月份重要科技硬體晶圓代工
龍頭台積電3Q26法說表示，受益於強勁AI需求與2
奈米開始貢獻營收，公司3Q26營收與獲利均優於市
場預期。3Q26高效運算與智慧手機營收比重分別達
68%、21%。高效運算營收年增高達 53%、智慧手
機年減10%。AI代理與開源模型普及帶動先進製程

用量 ， 台 積 電 預 估4Q26 美 元 營 收 將 年 增62% ，
2026年美元營收預估達44%。由於AI晶片成長高於
60%，預估台積電2027年營收與獲利仍將續創新高。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望
科技 正向 台積電、英特爾、類比晶片財報顯示資料中心需求仍遠優於消費電子終端，企業對於AI相關軟體支出持續增溫
• 雲端巨頭資本支出持平，伺服器
仍優於消費終端 Pg2
• 類比晶片終端需求上升 ，工業、
資料中心展望佳 Pg3
• 券商 CIO 調查顯示軟體支出預期
持續增溫 Pg4
• IT 支出首選AI/ML/自動化相關 Pg5

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
2
重點摘要
• 雲端巨頭資本支出持平 ，伺
服器仍優於消費終端
《雲端巨頭資本支出持平 ，伺服器仍優於消費終端 》
3Q26財報顯示 ，谷哥 、亞馬遜 、微軟與 Meta在
2026年資本支出目標合計達 7,300億美元 ，較上季
略減25億美元(-0.3%)，減少主要是微軟外部第三方
算力產能延遲導致資本支出目標下修 50億美元，與
Meta上調25億美元相互抵消 。雖然雲端巨頭支出
持平但資料中心晶片需求仍強勁。英特爾3Q26財報
顯示，受益於資料中心與 AI事業帶動營收達 165億
美元、年增21%。其中資料中心與人工智慧相關營
收73億美元續創歷史新高、年增78%，2027年相關
晶片仍將供不應求 。反觀個人電腦營收 81億美元、

年減5%。英特爾預估 2026年個人電腦出貨量將年
減12%、2027年受到記憶體與儲存供給影響預估將
續減10%。
受到上半年消費者預期終端售價上漲提前換機，
3Q26個人電腦與智慧手機出貨均呈現大幅下滑。根
據IDC統計，3Q26全球個人電腦出貨量達6,270萬
台，較2025年同期衰退20%。同樣受到記憶體漲價
與供應短缺衝擊的智慧手機3Q26出貨量2.6億台，
較2025年同期衰退22%。由於未來記憶體與儲存價
格小幅調漲以及供給受限下，消費終端價格仍將維
持高價位。因此，IDC預估個人電腦與智慧手機
2027年將高機率維持衰退，預估要2028年出貨量
才有望復甦。
資料來源：公司，2026/10/30，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/30

吸納量
重點摘要
3
《類比晶片終端需求上升 ，工業、資料中心占比較
高供應商營收更具成長性》
德州儀器(TXN)3Q26營收60.6億美元、EPS 2.62美
元雙雙優於預期，4Q26財測營收58-63億美元，中
位數較市場普遍預期高出 6%，反映資料中心市場
需求持續成長 ，使營收預測優於往年季節性回落 。
以3Q26終端市場來看 ，工業營收年增超過 40%、
季增約10%，成長來自航太國防 、能源基礎設施等
領域；車用營收年增約 15%、季增約10%，受益於
客戶庫存水準降低及電動車占比提高；資料中心營
收則年增約95%、季增20%，為成長最快的終端市
場。在營運方面，產能利用率提高以及2027年潛在
的價格調漲可能進一步擴大獲利成長空間 ，儘管資

本支出可能高於2026年的20~30億美元，但顯示終
端需求正擴大成長。
恩智浦(NXPI)3Q26營收37.9億美元、EPS 4.20美元
優於預期 ，但4Q26財測營收中位數 38.5億美元略
低於市場預期 。以3Q26終端市場來看 ，車用營收
20.7億美元、年增13%，成長主要來自軟體定義汽
車(SDV)產品貢獻；工業與物聯網營收 8.02億美元 、
年增39%，主要由資料中心 、工廠自動化和醫療保
健等產業帶動；通訊基礎設施營收 5.03億美元、年
增54%，得益於資料中心業務成長 。恩智浦財報結
果顯示各終端市場需求持續上升 ，惟成長速度較慢
的車用營收占比超過五成 ，未來成長速度可能不及
工業和資料中心營收占比更高的同業。
• 德州儀器 4Q26 財 測 營 收 較
市場預期高出6%，反映資料
中心市場需求大幅成長。
• 德儀產能利用率提高及 2027
年潛在的價格調漲可能進一
步擴大獲利成長空間。
• 恩智浦3Q26營收、EPS優於
市場預期 ，各終端市場營收
皆呈年增。

• 恩智浦 4Q26 財測低 於預期 ，
反映其成長速度較慢的車用
營收占比超過五成。

零售客流量表
• 券商 CIO 調查顯示 IT 採購預
算與軟體支出預期持續增溫。
• AI/ML 與網安軟體優先性持續
領先，但 ERP、BI、CRM 等
應用軟體優先性也見改善。
4
《券商 CIO 調查顯示軟體支出預期持續增溫》
券商 CIO 調查指出 2027 年的企業 IT 採購預算仍維
持成長。CIO 現在預計 2026 年的 IT 採購預算將年
增 4.0%，自 2Q26 走高 20bps，連續 3 次增溫。
對於 2027 年 IT 採購預算的首次估計，CIO 預期年
增 4.0%，雖僅與更新後的 2026 年預期相符，但實
則是近 4 年來的開年新高。在這之中，軟體預算成
長優於領先硬體、網通與服務，預期年增 4.4%，且

自 2026 年加速 10bps。2027 年軟體支出的預期加
速可能進一步說明短期內 AI 並未帶來全面性衝擊。
資料來源：摩根史丹利，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/20
人工智慧與機器學習 (AI/ML) 的支出優先性仍穩居
CIO 調查的第一名，佔比雖較前幾期調研回落，但
這應該是因為 CIO 對於開始增加其他項目的優先性。
AI 相關專案的預算仍最不可能被刪除，且淨占比
(即最不可能被刪除 – 最可能被刪除的比例)，3Q26
較 2Q26 大幅走揚 13ppts 至 17%。網路安全的支
出優先性則連 4 季走揚，且最不可能被刪除的淨占
比也走揚 1ppt 至 16%。持續反映出 AI 帶動的攻擊
面擴大、攻擊手法更加多元，與攻擊成本大幅走低，
導致企業在 AI 顯著產生正面效益前，優先選擇防範
未然的心態。而隨著 AI 的能力仍在快速發展，如近
期 Anthropic 研究即指出 AI 已可自我迭代，且小
型模型未來可能具備在物聯網裝置上自我複製的能

力，本行預期網安的需求可望仍持續強健。數位轉
型雖仍處第 3，但在本次券商調查中大幅地低於過
硬體 軟體 通訊設備 IT服務
■3Q25 ■4Q25 ■1Q26 ■2Q26 ■3Q26
2026 外部 IT 支出期望

### Page 4

3
中国
◼ 製造業PMIは拡大ペースが加速
９月のRatingDog製造業PMIは52.1と、
前月（51.5）から拡大ペースが加速。世
界的なハイテク・AIインフラ需要を背景
に電子機器などの新規受注が堅調に推移
した。
◼ インフレ率はわずかに加速
９月の総合CPIは前年比＋0.9%と、前月
（同＋0.8%）からわずかに加速したも
のの、依然として低水準で推移。輸送費
などが上昇した一方、豚肉価格を中心に
食料価格が引き続き下落し、総合CPIの
上昇を抑えた。
◼ 消費は低位で推移
８月の小売売上高は前年比＋0.4%にと
どまり、消費の回復ペースが鈍化。８月
の不動産販売面積は同▲13.0％と、依然
として減少が続いており、不動産市況の
低迷が長期化している。政府はリスク抑
制のため未完成物件の事前販売規制を強
化を打ち出している。
◼ 輸出は増加幅が拡大
９月の輸出は前年比+36.3%と、前月
（同+25.0%）から増加幅が拡大。世界
的なAI投資の拡大を背景に、半導体など
のハイテク製品や機械電気製品の出荷が

引き続き増加した。

◼ PMIは引き続き拡大
９月の総合PMIは55.9と、前月
（54.3）から拡大ペースが加速。製造
業PMI、サービス業PMIともに加速し、
ヘッドラインを押し上げた。
◼ インフレ率は加速
９月の総合CPIは前年比＋5.5%と、前
月（同＋4.8%）から加速。モンスー
ン期の局所的な豪雨や天候不順による
生鮮野菜の供給減少が食料インフレを
加速させた。
◼ 自動車販売は引き続き増加
９月の自動車販売台数は前年比＋
31.8％と、前月（同＋17.5％）に続き
増加。昨年９月前半にGST（物品サー
ビス税）減税発効前の買い控えがあっ
たベース効果に加え、農村部の需要が
回復したことで増加幅が拡大した。
◼ 鉱工業生産は引き続き増加
８月の鉱工業生産指数は前年比＋
8.0%と、前月（同＋7.4%）から拡大
ペースが加速。製造業部門や電力需要
の力強い稼働が、降雨による鉱業部門
の減速を補い、全体を大きく押し上げ
た。

◼ PMIは拡大ペースが加速

（52.3）から再び加速。AIインフラ向
け半導体および電子部品の国内外から
の新規受注が増加し、景況感が改善し
た。
◼ インフレは率は幾分鈍化
９月の総合CPIは前年比＋2.9%と、前
月（同＋3.1 %）から幾分鈍化。引き続
き輸送費が押し上げ要因となったもの
の、前月の携帯電話料金引き下げの裏
要因が剥落し、総合CPIを押し下げた。
◼ 消費は前月比で減少
８月の小売売上高は前期比▲1.8％と、
前月（同▲2.4％）に続き減少。猛暑や
物価、高金利上昇による実質可処分所
得の圧迫を受け、前月比で減少基調が
継続。高額耐久財や衣服などの裁量的
消費において家計の節約志向が意識さ
れている。
◼ 輸出は引き続き増加
９月の輸出は前年比＋83.7%と引き続
き増加。世界的なAI設備投資の拡大に
より、半導体輸出が同＋262.8%と、
前月（同＋209.1 %）に続き驚異的な伸
びを示し、輸出全体を牽引した。

◼ PMIは引き続き縮小

かに増加。週末を中心に香港住民が深
センなど中国本土へ越境して買い物を
行う「北上消費」の定着により、域内
の百貨店や外食チェーンの売上が伸び
悩んだ。

土を経由する先端半導体およびAI関連
ハイテク部材の荷動きが活発だったこ
とにより、前年比で底堅いプラス成長
を維持した。

前月（同＋2.9％）からわずかに鈍化。
いずれもインフレ目標のレンジ内に収
まって推移している。
◼ 消費は引き続き増加
８月の小売売上高は前年比＋1.3％と、
前月（同＋1.2％）に続き増加。９月
の中銀予測値は同＋1.4％と、引き続
き緩やかに増加する見通し。
◼ 輸出は引き続き増加

８月の輸出は前年比＋6.7％と、前月
（同＋6.0％）に続き増加。ニッケル
およびその派生・加工品の輸出が増加
し、輸出全体を牽引した。

特朗普被记者问及万斯的表态时说： “ 我认为这在很⼤程度上是真的，因为它（伊朗战事）

摘要：
美国副总统万斯 4 ⽇在社交媒体上发表⻓⽂，剖析共和党在中期选举中失利的原因，直⾔伊朗战
事是根源所在，并提出改变美国与以⾊列的关系。美国媒体解读，万斯在此节点上发⽂显然是
与美国总统特朗普做切割，开始为参加 2028 年总统竞选布局。
万斯在⽂章中写道，数据专家对他说， “ 很多 2024 年投票给特朗普和万斯的选⺠很愤怒，正打算
投给另⼀边 ” 。他认为， “ 接下来会有很多内⽃和争论，争论发⽣了什么、为什么会这样 …… 但我

们输掉的真正原因，⼏乎所有⼈都⼼知肚明。 ”
万斯坦⾔，他在中期选举前 “ 已经感觉不妙 ” ，并援引⺠调专家的观点提出让选⺠回归共和党的四
项任务，即结束伊朗战事、聚焦⺠众⽣活成本和通胀问题、坚持 “ 美国优先 ” 、改变美以关系。他
还直接点明， “ 我们失利的原因就是这场战争 …… 每个⼈都知道我们为什么输了 ” 。
当天晚些时候，特朗普被记者问及万斯的表态时说： “ 我认为这在很⼤程度上是真的，因为它
（伊朗战事）在短期内推⾼了汽油价格。 ” 他还说，从⼀开始就知道万斯对伊朗战事的看法。
美国有线电视新闻⽹⽂章分析认为，万斯的帖⽂ “ ⾮同寻常 ” ，包含对特朗普 “ 不加掩饰的批
评 ” ，他实际上是在与特朗普切割，以谋求能够在 2028 年竞选总统。万斯此举将 “ 成为特朗普时
代的⼀个转折点 ” 。
美国福克斯新闻频道报道说，⽩宫 4 ⽇上午召集政府官员开会，要求在中期选举后统⼀信息发布
⼝径，但 “ 万斯掌控了⾃⼰的信息发布，在外交政策和美以关系上与特朗普分道扬镳 ” 。报道援引

⼀名特朗普政府官员的话说，万斯提出的四项任务是他对美国 “ 新右翼 ” 发出的集结号，但他 “ 并
⾮像前副总统彭斯那样与特朗普决裂 ” 。 “ 新右翼 ” 的诉求被定义为不卷⼊海外冲突、让美国成为
⽣活可负担的地⽅以及关闭边境。

## Document 578: 中国人民银行上调外汇存款准备金率点评：政策传递稳汇率信号

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国人民银行上调外汇存款准备金率点评：政策传递稳汇率信号.pdf
Report source: 万联证券
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观快评报告

[Table_Title] 
政策传递稳汇率信号

[Table_ReportType] 
——中国人民银行上调外汇存款准备金率点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026年11月03日

[Table_Summary] 投资要点： 
⚫ 事件：11月 3日，中国人民银行发布公告称，将于11月
15日，将外汇存款准备金率由现行的4%提高到6%。 
⚫ 人民币持续升值，政策意在平抑单边升值预期。在美元指
数保持强势的背景下，人民币今年以来持续升值趋势，美
元兑人民币中间价在 11月初达到6.65。近期升值斜率走
陡，如果短期升值过快，可能会对出口企业造成一定汇兑
损失。9月底我国外汇存款余额为 1.2万亿美元，此次上
调将收回约240亿美元左右的流动性，有助于间接平抑短
期人民币升值斜率，降低市场对人民币单边升值的预期，
并减轻对出口企业的影响。 
⚫ 政策重在释放稳汇率信号，不改中期汇率走势。从历史上
外汇存款准备金工具使用来看：1）2021年5月由5%上调
至7%，12月由7%上调至9%，主要是抑制人民币单边升值
的趋势。2）2005年7月汇改后，人民币持续升值，在
2006年9月由 3%上调至4%，2007年5月上调至 5%。3）
在2022年4月、9月和 2023年9月逐步下调外汇存款准
备金率，来缓和人民币持续贬值的趋势。但从中期来看，
人民币汇率走势仍将回归基本面定价。 
⚫ 出口强势带动企业结汇意愿走强，人民币仍处升值通道当
中。我国经济保持稳健，出口强势，贸易顺差持续扩张，
企业结售汇意愿走强，央行外汇占款同比随之走高，财政
出口退税增长。我国与美元指数的驱动因素分化，两者呈
现双双走强的态势。 
⚫ 美元保持强势，AI行情逐步成为美元定向新变量。美元
指数10月以来维持高位震荡，下半年美元指数和油价相
关性逐步走弱，而AI产业发展推升了美国经济增长预
期，股票估值上行，海外资本流入，带动了美股、美债和
美元指数上行。若AI相关资产价格大幅调整，或带来美
元指数下跌。中期选举对外汇市场的直接影响预期有限。 
⚫ 人民币或受政策影响短期贬值，长期升值趋势不变。历史
上调升外汇存款准备金率后，人民币在短期内出现贬值，
市场对国内的增长预期边际修正。央行平抑人民币升值斜
率，有助于减轻出口导向型企业的汇兑损益压力，保护出
口企业的盈利预期，并且稳汇率有助于稳定外资配置盘的
预期。后续人民币走势仍将取决于经济数据表现，在年末

## Document 637: 凱基投顧_4915 致伸_余昀澄_20261105

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_4915 致伸_余昀澄_20261105.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 電子零組件 ‧ 台灣

致伸 
(4915.TW /4915 TT)

毛利率低於預期 
重要訊息  
3Q26 EPS  0.83 元低於我們預估。管理層表示隨著多項新專案啟動 ，車用與
AIoT 業務將成為 2027 年主要營收成長動能。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 EPS 低於我們預期。3Q26 EPS 年減 46%、季減 34%至 0.83 元，較
凱基及市場共識預估低 35%，主因毛利率不如預期。毛利率季減 1.4ppts、
年減 3.6 ppts 至 13.4%，較凱基預估低 1.2 ppts，主因零組件價格上漲、人
民幣升值，以及產品組合不利，其中智慧生活營收 比重由 2Q26 的 25%提
升至 3Q26 的 27%。而因研發費用較高，營利率 2%亦較我們 預估低
1.5ppts。1-3Q26 EPS 為 3.38 元，年減 29%。 
4Q26 毛利率展望低於預期。管理層展望 4Q26 營收年增低雙位數，隱含季
減高個位數，符合我們預期。因公共安全 (警用相機)  客戶需求強勁，4Q26
車用/AIoT 營收 (3Q26 營收比重 32%) 可望年增 20-30%，隱含季增低個位
數。智慧生活營收 (比重 27%) 展望年增逾 20%，隱含季減低個位數。 資訊
產品營收 (比重 41%) 則可能小幅年減、季減高個位數。然而，管理層預期零
組件漲價將持續壓抑 4Q26 毛利率，抵銷產品組合優化 (車用/AIoT 營收比重
提高) 之挹注。公司展望費用率小幅年減，高於我們原估，我們因此下修獲利
率及 4Q26 EPS 預估至 0.19 元，2026 年 EPS 下修至 3.56 元，年減 38%。 
車用/AIoT 為 2027 年主要成長動能， 但 1H27 毛利率仍面臨壓力。 管理層
目標將目前消費性產品為主 的營收結構  (凱基預估 2026 比重 65%)，逐步轉
型為以 B2B 業務為主。因此，致伸將擴大車用/ AIoT 產品布局，包括公共安
全、安防及機器人市場。公司 展望高毛利率 的公共安全與安防未來三年營收
CAGR 維持雙位數，並預計 2027 年為日本客戶組裝服務型機器人， 美國機
器人市場則以供貨相機模組為主。 上述將帶動車用/ AIoT 營收比重由 2026
年 34%提升至 2027 年 38% (營收金額年增 20%)。然而，考量消費性需求
疲弱，我們認為 消費產品與客戶的價格協商進度將 較先前預期緩慢。 1H27
毛利率仍將維持低檔，最快需待 2H27 才有機會回升。因此，我們下修 毛利
率預估，並下修 2027 年 EPS 預估至 4.34 元，年增 22%。 
投資建議 
因獲利下修，目標價調降至 52元，基於 12x 2027F EPS，維持「持有」評等。 
投資風險  
消費需求較弱、新專案與客戶遞延。  
持有‧維持

焦點內容

利率低於預期，下修 4Q26 EPS預估。 
3. Auto/AIoT 是 2027 年主要營收成長動
能。我們預期毛利率將於 2H27才逐步
回升，因此下修 2027年 EPS預估。

圖 6：公司概況  圖 7：B2B相機需求強勁，帶動3Q26車用/AIoT營收比重季增
至 32%。 
致伸成立於 1984 年，專門製造消費型與企業電子產品。 2025
年營收 49%來自資訊產品、32%來自車電/工業物聯網、 19%
來自智慧生活 ；電腦設計製造代工  (ODM) 與電子製造服務  
(EMS)兩種服務模式並存。近年產品組合再從手機相機鏡頭模
組(CCM)拓展至商務、監視錄像與汽車產品，並藉收購迪芬尼
(Tymphany) 切入音響及耳機市場。

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

## Document 638: 凱基投顧_6274 台燿_林祐熙_20261029

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_6274 台燿_林祐熙_20261029.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 印刷電路板 ‧ 台灣

台燿 
(6274.TW /6274 TT)

ASIC AI 伺服器客戶群將進一步擴大 
重要訊息 
公司公布 3Q26 季報，毛利率及營利率 36.9%、31%，優共識 31.8%、
25.9%，淨利 38.6 億，優於共識 15%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 毛利率及營利率表現亮眼，4Q26 高階產品開始調漲售價 。3Q26 高階
產品營收仍成長，但因中低階漲價幅度較大，導致 M7/M8 營收占比小幅下
降，加上泰國廠獲利 3Q26 轉盈，並彌補上半年虧損，使毛利率優預期。
M7/M8  (Networking 與 AI Server 占各半)、M4/M6  佔比分別季減 1、3 百分
點至 38%、28%；高壓材料 、Non-Low Loss，佔比季增 1、3 百分點至
15%、19%。4Q26 預期泰國廠仍可增加 10 萬張產能，而全系列 CCL 有漲
價機會，尤其高階 M7/M8 開始補漲，中低階因玻纖布供應緊張持續調漲、
泰國廠持續貢獻獲利， 有助改善產品組合與獲利。  
ASIC AI 伺服器正成為當前的核心成長引擎， 衛星與太空資料中心則開闢了
長期的發展藍海。摘要如下：  (1) AI Server 將 成為最大成長動能，新增數家
大型 ASIC 客戶，部分已進入送樣與板廠認證階段；而 美系 T3 伺服器持續有
供應商機，T4 預估 2Q27 有較明確規格及送樣進展。(2)  既有 400G Switch
客戶正加速認證 800G 及 1.6T Switch，部分客戶預期 2027 年 1.6T 需求可能
超越 800G，預期 Networking 客戶基礎較半年前顯著擴大。(3) 太空衛星板預
期 1H27 開始貢獻，材料 M8 以上。公司將逐步降低低階地面衛星板占比，
轉向高價值太空應用；客戶長期需求預測已延伸至 2032 年。(4) PTFE 公司提
出多種 Hybrid 解決方案，已有約 5-6 年研發基礎，並非新投入領域，但量產
設備仍需擴充，且受特殊高溫壓機產能限制，可能尋求外部合作。  
投資建議  
由於 3Q26 財報及 4Q26 展望優預期，我們上調 2027 獲利 34%，目標價上
調至 2070 元，相當於 2027 年每股盈餘 26 倍本益比 (先前為 32 倍)。 
投資風險  
客戶需求不如預期；競爭對手進度超前。  
增加持股‧維持

宏观日报 | 2026-11-06

中东战争、市场波动和欧洲加息 
市场分析 
宏观流动性： 
（01）美联储：12月加息概率78.8 %，较昨日+3.6个百分点。 
（02） 美元流动性： 美元指数101.5， 较昨日-0.3点；20日均值较昨日+0.0点，5日均值较昨日-0.1点。 比特币77803.9
点，较昨日+1093.9点；20日均值较昨日+488.2点，5日均值较昨日-198.2点。 
（03）货币流动性：美元SOFR/FFR利差20日均值-9.3BP，较昨日+0.4BP；5日均值-5.0BP，较昨日+0.4BP。人民币
DR007/OMO007利差20日均值-3.4BP，较昨日-0.3BP；5日均值-4.0BP，较昨日+1.4BP。

宏观经济： 
（04）商品市场：铜金比价20日均值3.53盎司/吨，较昨日+0.00；5日均值3.57盎司/吨，较昨日-0.03。油金比价20
日均值2.68盎司/100桶，较昨日+0.01；5日均值2.78盎司/100桶，较昨日-0.04。 
（05）债券市场：美债期限利差20日均值36.3BP，较昨日-0.8BP；5日均值34.6BP，较昨日-1.2BP。中债期限利差
20日均值42.1BP，较昨日-0.2BP；5日均值41.8BP，较昨日-0.4BP。德债期限利差20日均值37.0BP，较昨日-0.9BP；
5日均值33.4BP，较昨日-0.4BP。 
（06）股票市场：美股同比10.5%，较昨日-0.6个百分点；A股同比-11.1%，较昨日+0.1个百分点；欧股同比10.4%，
较昨日+3.2个百分点。

美国经济： 
（07）经济预期：经济意外指数20日均值44.1，较昨日+1.4；5日均值54.6，较昨日+2.1。 
（08）金融条件：金融条件指数20日均值-1.8，较昨日+0.0；5日均值-1.7，较昨日+0.1。

中国经济： 
（09）经济预期：经济意外指数20日均值-8.7，较昨日+2.1；5日均值8.2，较昨日+0.8。 
（10）碳排成本：CEA碳排放权20日均值96.4，较昨日+0.0；5日均值95.2，较昨日-0.2。 
（11）运输成本：内贸集装箱运价指数987.0，较上周-1.20%，较去年同期-8.95%。外贸集装箱进口运价指数118.1，
较上周-2.44%，较去年同期+18.63%。外贸集装箱出口运价指数157.5，较上周+0.30%，较去年同期+84.24%。 
（12）房地产：30城地产周度成交277.08万平，较上周+21.88%，较去年同期-11.27%。30城地产周度成交5周均值
230.44万平，较上周+5.90%，较去年同期-8.33%。

策略 
2026年的一场战争改写了中期选举的结果，而结果短期激起了一层涟漪。市场仍需要回答几个问题：选举前的战
争持续在选举后能否转变强度，降低通胀压力；共和党掌握两院下的科技扩张在选举后是否熄火，在自由现金流
和盈利下修后进行一次筹码换手。 我们认为利率在降低期限溢价之后， 中期选举是一次“踩刹车”而非“掉头”， 前者
英国央行再次加息，等待后者的证伪信号（11.17NVDA财报） 。建议继续关注11月风险释放后的机会。

## Document 759: 民主党“第一波报复”：美防长最早下周面临“弹劾投票”

Source PDF: all report pdf/民主党“第一波报复”：美防长最早下周面临“弹劾投票”.pdf

### Page 1

共和党众议员 Massie 将于下周重新提出弹劾赫格塞斯动议，核⼼指控为其未经授权发动对
伊战争。此次动议有望获⺠主党全⼒⽀持。 Massie 警告，这不过是 “ 温和预演 ”—— ⺠主党
明年接管国会后将发起更猛烈攻势。
摘要：
伊朗战争令共和党在中期选举中惨败，美国国防部⻓赫格塞斯（ Pete Hegseth ）随之成为两党
交锋的最新靶⼼。国会最快下周将就其弹劾动议展开⾏动，这场政治⻛暴正迅速升级。
据彭博社 11 ⽉ 6 ⽇报道，共和党众议员 Thomas Massie 表⽰，他将在国会下周复会后 “ 肯定 ” 重
新提出弹劾条款，核⼼指控为赫格塞斯在未经国会授权的情况下发动对伊战争。此次弹劾动议
预计将获得⺠主党⼈的⼴泛⽀持，并可能吸引⾜够多的共和党⼈联署，具备通过⻔槛。
弹劾动议⼀旦在众议院通过，将迫使参议院⽴即启动耗时漫⻓的弹劾审判程序，除⾮共和党能
够集票将议案搁置。⽽在选⺠刚刚就伊朗战争对共和党发出惩罚性信号之后，这⼀程序性投票
将把每位议员的⽴场公开摆上台⾯。特朗普周四表⽰，赫格塞斯做了 " 出⾊的⼯作 " ，并坚称没有

将其免职的计划。
Massie 警告：⺠主党掌权后将发起更猛烈攻势
Massie 在周四的社交平台帖⼦中明确表⽰，其弹劾动议不过是 “ 预演 ” 。 “ 我的弹劾决议，与⺠主
党掌控国会后将发起的攻势相⽐，只是温和版本。他们将举⾏听证会和审判， ” 他写道。
Massie 本⼈在初选中败北 —— 特朗普为其对⼿背书是重要因素 —— 他同时建议特朗普尽快解雇
赫格塞斯，以便由共和党仍控制的参议院完成对继任者的确认。他指出，⼀旦⺠主党于明年 1 ⽉
接管参议院，确认程序将⼤幅增加难度。
Hegseth 周四在 X 平台发布了⼀段六分钟的视频，为⾃⼰的任期进⾏辩护，并将 Massie 形容为
“ ⼼怀不满的落选议员 ” ，称其提出的是 “ 针对本⼈的虚假弹劾条款 ” 。

1 条评论
伊朗战争成政治包袱，赫格塞斯处境孤⽴
赫格塞斯⾯临的政治压⼒，根源在于他作为伊朗战争主要内阁责任⼈的⻆⾊。这场战争在中期
选举中严重拖累了共和党选情。华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道，副总统万斯（ JD Vance ）周三在 X 平台
直接将共和党中期选举惨败归咎于这场冲突，措辞罕⻅直⽩：
赫格塞斯在国会⼭⼏乎没有盟友可⾔。整个战争期间，他与国会的关系持续恶化，原因包括多
次对抗性听证会，以及被指责拒绝向监督委员会共享信息 —— 包括有关战争成本的信息。
据报道，赫格塞斯⾃获提名之初便争议不断。这位前国⺠警卫队成员、福克斯新闻前主持⼈，
在提名阶段就遭到议员们的强烈质疑，被批评为缺乏领导世界最强⼤军队的资质与能⼒。他最
终以极微弱的优势获得参议院确认，整个任期内始终依赖特朗普的公开背书维系地位。

此次 Massie 提出弹劾，并⾮⾸次 —— 他此前已就伊朗战争未经国会授权⼀事提出过相关动议。
值得注意的是，在中期选举中落败的共和党议员，在 “ 跛脚鸭 ” 会期⼏乎没有理由返回华盛顿投
票，这意味着⽀持弹劾的票数基础可能因此进⼀步扩⼤。
“ 我们失败的原因就是这场战争。如果你想争辩这是值得的 —— 为此输掉未来六年的参议院多数
席位是值得的 —— 那就说吧。 ”

## Document 820: 米国：消費・ＡＩ投資主導で年率＋3.4％へ加速（26年7-9月期ＧＤＰ) ～力強い内需と高止まりするインフレが継続、ＦＲＢの追加利上げを後押し～

26/3Q ＋3.4 ＋3.8 ▲3.3 ＋12.7 (＋1.61) ＋5.3 (▲3.32) ▲5.7 ＋19.7 ＋5 .6 ＋2.3

実質ＧＤＰ（Gross Domestic Product） 
米国：消費・ＡＩ投資主導で年率＋3.4％へ加速（26 年 7-9 月期ＧＤＰ) 
～力強い内需と高止まりするインフレが継続、ＦＲＢの追加利上げを後押し～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治 
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・2026 年 7-9 月期の実質GDP成長率（1 次推計）は前期比年率＋3.4％（4-6 月期：＋ 
2.2％）へ加速し、市場予想（＋3.2％）を上回った。実質民間国内最終需要が＋4.9％と大幅に 
伸びを加速させた。 
・需要項目別では、長期金利上昇等の影響で住宅投資が減少に転じ、フリート需要が一巡したも 
のの、生成AI普及に伴うデータセンター・サーバー等の整備需要が加速し、情報関連機器が＋ 
23.2％と急増した。また、CHIPS法やIRA（インフレ抑制法）などの政策効果による製造業の国 
内回帰も寄与し、設備投資全体で＋12.7％と高い伸びを維持した。個人消費（＋3.8％）も、サ 
ッカー北米W杯特需や資産効果等に支えられ、サービス消費主導で力強く推移した。 
・労働市場の需給均衡が続く一方、コアPCEデフレーターは前年同期比＋3.1％と高止まりして 
おり、ＦＲＢのインフレ目標（2％）に向けたインフレ低下ペースの鈍さ（粘着性）が顕著であ 
る。背景には堅調な賃金の伸びとサービス価格の粘着性がある。 
・成長の強さとインフレの粘着性を受け、年内の追加利上げおよび高金利の長期化（Higher for 
Longer）が見込まれる。年内あと 1 回（25bp）の追加利上げとターミナルレート（4.25〜
4.50％）の見通しが高まることで、2027 年以降は高金利の累積的効果に伴う成長鈍化が予想さ 
れる。

### Page 2

2/7 
1．GDP 成長率の加速（年率＋ 3.4 ％）と強固な内需  
2026 年 7-9 月期の米国実質GDP成長率（1 次推計）は、前期比年率＋3.4％と市場予想 
（Bloomberg集計）の同＋3.2％を上回り、4-6 月期の同＋2.2％から加速した（前年同期比は＋
2.1％と 4-6 月期の＋2.2％からほぼ横ばい） 。また、実質民間国内最終需要が前期比年率＋4.9％
（4-6 月期：同＋4.6％）へとさらに加速したことで、ヘッドラインの数値以上に米国経済の国内 
需要の強さが示された。

2．AI インフラ・製造業投資主導の設備投資と力強いサービス消費  
需要項目別では、住宅投資は長期金利の上昇や建設業者のマインド悪化などが続くなかで、前
期比年率▲3.3％（4-6 月期：同＋2.8％）と再び減少した。 
一方、設備投資が前期比年率＋12.7％（4-6 月期：同＋9.0％）へと高い伸びに加速した。内訳 
をみると、フリート需要の沈静化や供給制約によって輸送用機器は同▲7.9％（同＋24.6％）とマ
イナスに転じたものの、商業ビル・オフィスなどの非住宅構築物投資は同＋5.4％（同＋0.1％）
とプラス幅を拡大した。さらに、生成AIブームに伴うデータセンターやサーバー整備需要に支え 
られ、情報関連機器が同＋23.2％（同＋10.7％）と再び高い伸びを示すなど、AI関連投資は高水 
準を維持した。ハイパースケール企業（大手ITプラットフォーマー）による巨額の設備投資が、
ハードウェアおよびインフラ需要を強力に押し上げている。また、製造業の国内回帰政策（CHIPS 
法やIRA関連投資の進捗）による拠点新設に伴って産業用機器が同＋24.7％（同＋27.0％） 、知的 
財産が同＋8.4％（同＋9.2％）と高い伸びを維持した。 
個人消費に関しても前期比年率＋3.8％に強まり、4-6 月期の同＋3.8％に続き高い伸びを維持し
た。品目別では、自動車などの耐久財が鈍化した一方、インフレ率の落ち着きや値引き・セール 
等による実質購買力の維持を背景に、食品・衣服などの非耐久財が底堅く推移した。さらに、堅
調な雇用と実質賃金の増加に支えられ、住宅・公共料金、飲食・宿泊、輸送などの伸びが寄与し 
てサービス消費が大幅に拡大した。なお、6 月中旬から 7 月上旬にかけて開催されたサッカー北米 
W杯に伴うチケット販売や外食・宿泊、輸送などでの一時的な特需も、サービス消費を押し上げる 
要因となった。 
これらを受けて、実質民間国内最終需要が前期比年率＋4.9％（4-6 月期：同＋4.6％）へとさら 
に加速しており、米国経済の国内需要は力強さを増している。他方、政府支出が同＋5.3％（同▲
0.1％）とプラスに転じたことで、これらを合わせた実質国内最終需要は同＋5.0％（同＋3.8％） 
と大幅に加速した。 
なお、在庫投資寄与度は同＋1.61％ポイント（4-6 月期：▲0.53％ポイント）とプラス転換した 
が、純輸出寄与度は、内需の強さを背景とした輸入急増やドル高・海外経済の減速による輸出減
少により同▲3.32％ポイント（同▲1.10％ポイント）へ悪化した。両者あわせて成長率を▲
1.71％ポイント押し下げている。

米国労働市場（四半期） （千人） 
（出所）米労働省より作成

3．労働市場の安定 と根強いインフレ圧力  
労働市場では、同期の民間雇用者数が月平均 7.7 万人増と緩やかな増加にとどまったが、失業 
率は 4.3％と低い水準にとどまっており、労働需給の均衡が続いた。一方で、AI活用等による生産 
性の向上が潜在成長率を一定程度押し上げ、急激な賃金インフレのスパイラルを抑制する効果も 
一部で観測される。 
インフレ環境では、7-9 月期のPCE（個人消費支出）デフレーターが前期比年率＋2.1％（4-6 月
期：＋5.0％）と伸びが鈍化した。一方、コアPCEデフレーターも前期比年率＋2.5％（同＋ 
3.3％）と鈍化したものの高止まりしている。 
前年同期比で見るとPCEデフレーターが＋3.5％（4-6 月期：＋3.6％）とわずかな低下にとどま 
り、コアPCEデフレーターが＋3.1％（同＋3.1％）と横ばいで推移するなど、インフレの粘り強さ 
が顕著である。特に住居費を除くコアサービスインフレの根強さが続いており、FRBが目標とする 
2％への早期回帰が困難であることを示している。

4．経済成長の基調 は堅調さを 維持  
米国の経済成長の基調を 3 四半期移動平均でみると、7-9 月期の実質GDPは前期比年率＋2.7％
（4-6 月期：同＋1.6％）と加速した。純輸出や在庫といったボラティリティの大きい要因の影響 
を除いた実質国内最終需要の移動平均は同＋3.7％（同＋2.2％）へと加速しており、堅調な個人 
消費を主因として内需の強さが一層明確になっている。

5．FRB の高金利長期化 政策と今後の成長減速見通し  
今回の結果は、米国経済の力強い拡大と粘り強いインフレを証明しており、FRBの追加利上げ判
断を後押しする内容となった。強い成長とコアデフレーターの高止まりは、年内の追加利上げお 
よび政策金利の高水準維持（Higher for Longer）を正当化すると予想される。今後は、この高金 
利の継続や累積的な引き締め効果が、個人消費への負荷や住宅・民間設備投資への抑制圧力とな 
り、経済成長を徐々に抑制するとみられる。

## Document 850: 美联储鹰声再起！Hammack称利率需升至限制性水平，倾向于更频繁、小幅加息

Source PDF: all report pdf/美联储鹰声再起！Hammack称利率需升至限制性水平，倾向于更频繁、小幅加息.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美联储鹰派官员 Hammack 表⽰，通胀已连续近六年超标，劳动⼒市场出现温和⾛强迹象，
主张 " 更频繁、更⼩幅度 " 加息，警告通胀预期⼀旦固化将⼤幅抬⾼美联储施策成本。她同时
⼒挺美联储独⽴性，将其与美元地位直接挂钩。市场⽬前预计美联储将在 12 ⽉和明年 3 ⽉分
别加息⼀次。
摘要：
美联储官员释放鹰派信号，主张以更频繁的⼩幅加息对抗持续超标的价格压⼒。
克利夫兰联储主席 Beth Hammack 周四表⽰，货币政策需要转向限制性⽴场，才能及时将通胀拉
回 2% ⽬标。她同时指出，通胀已连续近六年⾼于⽬标，价格压⼒具有⼴泛基础，受供需双重因
素驱动。
Hammack 上周是两位在利率决议中投出异议票的联储官员之⼀。在联储按兵不动后，她在苏黎
世的⼀场活动中重申⽴场，倾向于 " 尽可能采取更频繁、更⼩幅度的调整 "，称这⼀策略有助于降
低政策落后于形势、以及通胀预期根深蒂固的⻛险。
同时，⾥⼠满联储主席 Tom Barkin 周四另⾏发表讲话，重申⽀持 " 渐进式 " 加息步伐，但拒绝就

利率最终将升⾄何处作出表态。⾃上次会议以来发⾔的多位联储官员也均倡导以温和节奏推进
紧缩，以限制对⾦融市场和实体经济的冲击。
期货市场定价显⽰，投资者⽬前预计美联储将在 12 ⽉和明年 3 ⽉分别加息⼀次。
鹰派异⻅： Hammack 主张限制性利率⽴场
Hammack 在此前的会议上是少数派。上周议息会议，联储以多数票决定维持基准利率不变，
Hammack 与另⼀位官员投票反对，坚持认为当前通胀形势要求更为紧缩的政策取向。
她周四重申，通胀已持续⾼于⽬标近六年，且价格压⼒呈现出⼴泛性，并⾮局限于某⼀特定领
域或单⼀因素。
她表⽰，重复出现的供给冲击将加⼤企业和消费者形成通胀预期的⻛险，⼀旦通胀思维固化，
央⾏实现价格稳定⽬标的难度将显著上升。
在加息节奏上， Hammack 表达了对⼩步快⾛策略的偏好，认为相较于⼤幅度、低频次的调整，
更频繁的⼩幅加息能够让政策更贴近经济现实，避免被动追赶局⾯。
在会议安排上，美联储主席沃什上周宣布计划在 2028 年前调整年度议息⽇程，将正式利率决策

会议从每年⼋次减⾄六次，另留两次会议专⻔讨论宏观货币政策框架。 Hammack 和 Barkin 均对
此淡然处之。" 从⼋次到六次，这是⼀个相当温和的转变， "Hammack 说。
劳动⼒市场出现温和⾛强迹象
在就业⽅⾯， Hammack 的评估相对乐观，认为⻓期处于低招聘、低裁员僵局的美国劳动⼒市场
可能正在出现边际改善。
" 从就业这⼀⽬标来看，我看到的经济运⾏状况相当不错， " 她说，并补充称看到了 " 劳动⼒市场
潜在⾛强的⼀些温和迹象 " 。

有限。
央⾏独⽴性与美元地位直接挂钩
Hammack 在演讲中还就美元霸权地位与央⾏独⽴性发表看法，将两者直接挂钩。她表⽰，美元
的主导地位依赖于美国健全的法治体系、美元资产在深度和流动性市场中的可得性，以及央⾏
的独⽴运作。
她明确强调，联储通过⾃⾝⾏动，已建⽴起基于证据和经济数据、⽽⾮短期政治压⼒来制定货
币政策的机构公信⼒， " 这种公信⼒帮助我们实现⽬标，包括低⽽稳定的通胀，同时⽀撑美元地
位。 "
Hammack 发表上述⾔论时，距美国中期选举结果揭晓仅两天。此次选举中，⺠主党赢得国会两
院控制权，外界认为此结果可能在⼀定程度上缓和特朗普对联储利率决策施压的能⼒。

稳定币扩张路径： AI 或成关键变量
Hammack 还就数字资产趋势发表了罕⻅表态，将⼈⼯智能的崛起列为稳定币⼤规模普及的潜在
催化剂。
她指出，稳定币⽬前的使⽤范围仍然有限，但如果⾕歌、亚⻢逊等拥有庞⼤现有⽤⼾⽹络的科
技巨头推出⾃有代币，市场规模可能迅速扩张。与此同时，⾃主运⾏的 AI 系统也可能推动稳定币
使⽤量⼤幅提升。
" 如果越来越多的 AI 代理需要进⾏买卖交易，⽽它们开始使⽤稳定币或其他数字资产，⽽⾮通过
信⽤卡或传统⽀付渠道处理资⾦，这可能构成⼀种实质性转变， " 她说， " 我们⽬前还没有看到这
些应⽤场景，但我们正在密切关注。 "

全球科技競逐白熱化，中國佈局馬不停蹄

市場對川習會所建立的 「超級智慧溝通機制」 實際成效持審慎態度， 預期美中科
技競爭的長期格局不會改變 。 科技自主與國家安全考量下 ， 中國將持續以政策支
持、財政補貼及產業扶植措施，推動科技供應鏈自主可控與核心技術國產化進
程。

本期內容： 
1. 美中 AI 對話機制或
流於無效溝通 
2. 美中競逐反加速各
國 AI 佈局 
3. 中國科技業發展獲
政策、補貼支援

重點摘要 
■ 美中將建立超級智慧熱線 ，但回顧過
往美中兩國熱線電話的歷史，雙方在
危機時刻並未能產生立即性的有效溝
通。市場期待美中雙方溝通 應改採更
可行的對話機制。

■ 市場分析美國可望於明年初步建立自
主稀土供應，屆時可能恢復對中國施
壓。美中看似聯手 AI 風險控管又同時
互相掣肘 ， 仍不改產業長線發展方向 ，
且各國普遍加碼投入 AI 發展下，瑞銀
預估全球 AI 資本支出規模將不斷攀升。

■ 為突破美國在科技上的制肘措施，今
年中國十五五規劃以及新質生產力均
顯示政府全面推動科技自主的戰略方
向，且未來五年內 AI 基礎建設計畫下，
中國本土半導體與 AI 晶片產業將成為
最大政策受益者。

▋ 川習會科技議題高舉輕放 
9 月川習會前市場高度關注美中針對晶片
或尖端科技議題 ， 進行技術和供應鏈管制等
相關主題的討論，然而最終僅將 AI 重新定
義為超級智慧(Super Intelligence)、 設立AI
雙邊對話管道等風險控管議題。美中在科技
敏感議題上避重就輕 ， 藉此換取貿易戰休兵
延長至 2027 年初。

美國科技新聞網站 The Information報導，
中國政府可能允許字節跳動及阿里巴巴等
大型中國科技企業購買一款輝達供高階 AI
推論與工作站應用設計的新晶片。 該晶片並
非專門針對大型資料中心訓練的高階 AI 晶
片，而未受美國現行出口管制規範 ， 在中國
方面雖尚無採購數量 、 時間表及審批標準的
具體訊息 ， 不過若中國政府放行企業採購管
制，為大型科技企業提供及時的硬體支撐，
或可視為美中晶片管制措施上具政策彈性 。

▋ 美中競逐發展超級智慧 
美中超級智慧熱線旨在建立直接溝通管道 ，
以應對駭客攻擊 、 國家安全疑慮或失控等潛
在問題， 但若回顧過往美中兩國熱線電話的
歷史，因美中文化或戰略歧異 (美方傾向即
時通話避免衝突升級 ， 中方高度集權使下屬
沒有取得最高領導人授權下不能表態 )而使
雙方在危機時刻並未能透過電話產生立即
性的有效溝通 。 市場期待美中雙方溝通仍有
待長期磨合並改採更可行(如文字溝通方式)
的對話機制。(圖二) 
此外 ， 市場分析美國可望於明年初步建立自
主稀土供應 ， 屆時可能恢復對中國施壓，並
將執行嚴格管制和封鎖 ， 美中將持續在尖端
科技上競爭 ， 並各自拉攏盟友推動全球形成
兩套不同的科技和供應鏈體系 。 即便美中在
超級智慧 發展上看似聯手風險控管又同時
互相掣肘下 ， 仍不改產業長線發展方向 ， 且
在各國普遍加碼投入 AI 發展下，瑞銀預估
全球 AI 資本支出今年上看 9,980 億美元，
明年支出規模將不斷攀升。(圖三)

圖二：歷史上美中熱線未達成即時有效溝通 
事件 結果 
2001 年中美南
海撞機事件 
美軍 EP-3 偵察機與中國戰機相
撞 ， 美方試圖聯繫中方 ， 但初期無
法取得即時且有效的高層對話。 
2009 年無瑕號
對峙事件 
中國海軍在南海國際水域包圍美
國監測船無瑕號 ， 五角大廈撥打危
機熱線 ，經 24 小時後才正式通話 。 
2023 年間諜氣
球被擊落事件 
美國空軍擊落中國高空探測氣球 ，
美方透過美中軍事特別危機專線
致電中國，但中方拒絕通話。 
資料來源：北富銀彙整，2026/10/5

▋ 中國官方力推科技自主 
上半年中國全國人大四中全會批准並公佈
實施第十五個五年規劃綱要 ，將高水平科技
自立自強列為首要核心目標，規劃全社會研
發經費年均成長率維持超過 7%，以及先前
提出的新質生產力(以科技創新為主導的高
階生產力推動產業發展)均顯示政府推動科
技自主的戰略方向 ， 而美國在科技上的掣肘
措施 ， 更加刺激中國官方加速建立本土自主
可控的 AI 生態與供應鏈替代的政策方向，
以突破美國的晶片與技術禁令， 預期中國科
技產業發展仍將持續獲政策或補貼支持。

在 AI 基礎建設計畫方面，未來五年內中國
規劃編列 5 兆元人民幣投資於新型電網，
預計將拉動特高壓 、 智慧電網及配電網升級，
還有投入 2 兆元人民幣整合全國數據中心
資源，以實現算力統一調度等一系列基礎建
設計畫 ， 可望支援未來中國城市中自動駕駛 、
無人機和智慧城市的龐大算力需求。

受到美國持續收緊高階晶片出口管制 ，此次
建設中國官方明確指定至少 80%必須採用
國產晶片與技術設備，以提高中國 AI 晶片
自給率，中國本土半導體與 AI 晶片產業將
成為最大政策受益者，華為 、 寒武紀等中國
本土算力晶片可望加速技術迭代與商業化。
高盛預期中國國產AI 晶片出貨量將從2025
年 110 萬顆、2026 年 240 萬顆、2027 年
480 萬顆 ， 並攀升至 2030 年 1,100 萬顆 ，
市場規模將有望從人民幣 940 億元成長至
人民幣 6,460 億元。此外，中國伺服器、散
熱溫控等上下游產業鏈， 以及電網相關建設
廠商亦將受惠。(圖四)

圖四：中國國產 AI 晶片出貨量看增 
資料來源：GS，2026/9/26

▋ 結論 
美中在科技與政治上的角力 ，不但沒有阻礙
AI 產業的發展 ， 反而使競爭白熱化 ， 成為推
動中國加速技術突破與產業擴張的 強力催
化劑。為爭奪未來數十年的科技主導權 ，中
美雙方傾注國力提供政策支援或政府補貼
推動 AI 發展，民間企業如微軟、Google、
Meta、百度、騰訊、阿里等科技巨頭提出
史無前例的資金規模投入 AI 研發與算力基
礎設施。在晶片和演算法軍備競賽中 ， 中國
除了在發達的製造業領域 ， 還有自動駕駛 、
無人機等應用場景中全方位加速 AI 深度融
入，可望挹注中國科技業長線增長動能。

2. 短端利率具備下行

重點摘要 
■ 3Q26 金融股財報已率先揭露，截至
10/15，整體盈餘優於預期的比重僅
70%，低於過去十年的 77%，尤其大
型金融股 ， 多數僅符合預期 ， 引來短線
上失望性賣壓。(如圖一)

■ 9 月短端殖利率大幅飆升 ， 引發長短利
差收窄，然而 9 月通膨數據超預期卻
未再次令殖利率走高，市場定價已提
前反應 ， 若後續通膨走勢走緩 ， 市場有
再次修正升息預期空間，有利長短利
差再次擴大。

■ 隨著金融股價修正，目前的估值位階
已來到 2024 年 AI 多頭行情以來的相
對低位 ， 儘管私人信貸違約風險上升 ，
然而擴大至系統性風險機率不高，整
體衝擊程度有限。

台北市大安區仁愛路四段 169 號

▋ 美國金融股財報具韌性 
身為民生經濟的窗口與樞紐 ， 銀行股的營運
動 能 一直 是 景氣 變化 的觀 察 指標 。 截至
10/14，摩根大通、花旗、高盛、富國銀行
已率先揭露 3Q26 財報 ， 除了高盛營收明顯
優於預期之外 ， 其餘金融股業績大致符合預
期 ， 甚至出現低於預期的情況 ， 導致股價在
財報公布後，率先下跌反應。(如圖二)

細項觀察 ， 整體表現並沒有想像中差勁 ， 以
摩根大通為例 ，3Q26 營收 509 億美元低於
預期 513 億美元的關鍵，主要源於固收業
務的下滑，反應Q3 全球殖利率飆升下，市
場對於債券交易的熱度出現暫時性放緩 ， 其
餘業務包含利息收入 、 股權交易和投行收入
皆符合預期。摩根大通上調 2026 全年 NII
至 1,062 億美元(原先預估為 1,055 億美元)，
並預期 2027 年將進一步上升至 1,070 億
美元 ， 同時花旗也將全年ROTCE(有形普通
股權益報酬率 )由先前的 10~11%上調至
11%以上 ， 儘管上調幅度皆不是特別明顯 ，
但結合備抵呆帳的穩定 ， 意味大型金融業對
於景氣前景依舊偏正向看待。

圖二：大型金融股財報並不差

資料來源：Bloomberg/北富銀整理，2026/10/14  
▋ 短端利率具備下行空間 
聯準會 9 月點陣圖顯示 2026 年再升息一
碼後，2027 年預期維持利率不變，儘管 9
月美國通膨出現略為上升的勢頭 ， 但由於2
年期短端殖利率先前已反應未來 1 年仍有
3 碼升息的可能性，8~9 月 10-2 年期長短
利差一度下跌近 40bp，9 月通膨數據公布
後短端利率並未上升。

市場可能過度高估聯準會升息的幅度 ， 聯準
會紐約聯儲總裁 Williams 10 月談話也表
示，今年或再升息一次，目前認為2027 年
沒有升息必要 。 此前市場一度預期未來仍有
4 碼升息空間，如今已修正為3 碼 ，後續甚
至有進一步下滑可能 ， 或推動短端殖利率持
續走低 。 與此同時 ， 長債端有政府債務壓力
干擾 ， 儘管川普政府試圖利用經濟成長 ， 降
低債務/GDP 比重 ， 但以2027 年 2.3~2.5%
的 GDP 成長預估而言，難以說服市場，長
端殖利率不易下行 ， 整體長短利差有望繼續
陡峭化。(如圖三)

圖三：短率飆升衝擊銀行股 9 月走勢

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/14

▋ 價值浮現，惟關注私人信貸風險 
隨著股價回落 ，S&P500 金融指數估值也同
步降至 14.5X， 位於5 年均值之下 ， 同時也
是 2024 年股市開啟 AI 多頭行情以來的相
對低位。尤其資本市場次產業預期PE 偏離
5 年均值的幅度達 8%，更為明顯，主要受
到 私 人 信 貸 市 場 惡 化 衝 擊 ， 包 含Ares 
Management(ARES)、Blackstone(BX)、
KKR 集團(KKR)股價都在 9 月份出現明顯跌
幅。(如圖四)

信評機構 KBRA 監控的私人信貸直接貸款
雷達於 9 月份出現惡化跡象，172 家被示
警紅燈(對應一般信評標準為 CCC 及以下
評等)，96 家為橘燈(BB 或 B 評等)， 整體示
警比例也由 8 月的 8.1%攀升至 8.8%。(如
圖五)

儘管私人信貸違約風險成為市場不確定因
素 ， 然而以私人信貸業務為主的市值占比並
不高，ARES、BX、KKR 三家合計占資本市
場次產業比重約 12%，占整體金融指數的
比重也僅 3.2%，因此規模上影響不大。

圖四：金融股估值位階跌至均值下方

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/14 
圖五：私人信貸風險正在上升

資料來源：JPM，2026/10/12 
▋ 結論 
財報未超預期 ， 引來失望性賣壓 ， 但檢視大
型金融股財報 ， 表現上也並沒有想像中的差 ，
甚至股權交易和投行業務收入高於預期 。 換
句話說 ， 在業績未見惡化 ， 但股價卻向下修
正下，投資的價值正慢慢浮現。

Coreweave 借貸成本飆升，引爆私人信貸
風險 ， 雖須留意相關影響 ， 但目前觀察擴大
致系統性風險機率不高 。 高盛預估2027 年
具相似性質槓桿貸款違約率雖維持約 4.2%
的高檔 ， 但不會進一步攀升 ， 此外KBRA 指
數顯示 ， 私人信貸雖不透明 ， 但平均回收率
47.1%， 高於槓桿貸款的39%和非投等債的
37.9%， 實際衝擊未必如同市場情緒恐慌 。

*所列個股為舉例說明，並非個股推薦
